| Objective | Journal path |
| --- | --- |
| Wait for Delamain to call. (Primary) | `quests/side_quest/sq025c07_suicidal/07_find_suicidal/071w_wait_suidial` |
| Ahh, the Glen. You know this used to be a college campus? Some thought that taking out colossal loans and enrolling in a biotech degree would make their lives better. Then the harsh reality kicked in – it was easier to throw yourself off a bridge than get into a corporate research lab. Makes you wonder why our rogue Delamain cab chose this place of all places. (Primary) | `quests/side_quest/sq025c07_suicidal/07_find_suicidal/07_find_suicidal` |
| Approach the Delamain cab. (Primary) | `quests/side_quest/sq025c07_suicidal/07_find_suicidal/07a_scan_suicidal` |
| Stay within signal range. (Primary) | `quests/side_quest/sq025c07_suicidal/07_find_suicidal/07b_disable_suicidal` |
| Get back close to the Delamain cab. (Primary) | `quests/side_quest/sq025c07_suicidal/07_find_suicidal/07c_return_suicidal` |
| Talk to Delamain. (Primary) | `quests/side_quest/sq025c07_suicidal/07_find_suicidal/07x_complete_suicidal` |

World NodeRef definitions/streaming owners, full actor and animation closure, numeric TweakDB names, spoken subtitle/VO payload joins and installed/save behavior have not been validated by this structural pass. Raw direct references, actor bindings, choices, event locstrings and community entries remain in the evidence shards.

## Required acceptance cases before reuse

Exercise normal completion and each named alternate outcome; interrupt and return at every scene handoff; start with spatial/item/actor predicates already satisfied; save/load across waits and joins; cancel parallel listeners; verify reward delivery once; and verify cleanup without changing the committed outcome. Apply the block-specific cases in the [expanded contracts](../expanded-blocks.md). No installed playtest is claimed.
